# slew/swing/power passive-corner study (RZ x CC) -- record 20261123-213924-a25bbbc

- **Date (UTC)**: 2026-11-23 21:39:24
- **Issue**: #97 (DR-3 section (d) obligation; replaces the analytic slew scaling of the gain/GBW/PM passive study with a measurement); #150 (full 45-point envelope)
- **Scope**: `full` -- FULL operating envelope: 5 MOS corners x 3 temperatures x 3 supplies = 45 points, each figure over the passive axes it responds to; cells per figure: slew 405, swing 135, power 45 (585 of the uniform 3x3's 1215)
- **Record kind**: SIDE STUDY. It does not judge, replace or supersede any aggregate-report row; the default 45-point grid records (typical passives) stay authoritative: `20261010-161852-0127eb7`. No spec row or bound is edited here.
- **Verdict against the ratified bounds (`spec/target-spec.md` Sec.2), measured (not analytic) per RZ x CC cell**:
  - **Quiescent power: PASS** vs ratified <= 350 uW -- passes at 45/45 cells (fails at 0); worst 311 uW at ff / -40 C / 3.63 V, RZ typical, CC typical.
  - **Slew rate (slower of rise/fall, CL = 2 pF): PASS** vs ratified >= 10 V/us -- passes at 405/405 cells (fails at 0); worst 14.25 V/us at ss / 125 C / 2.97 V, RZ best, CC worst.
  - **Output swing: PASS** vs ratified >= 2.3 Vpp -- passes at 135/135 cells (fails at 0); worst 2.465 Vpp at ss / 125 C / 2.97 V, RZ best, CC typical.

## Conditions

- **PDK**: /home/ubuntu/.ciel/gf180mcuD (open_pdks f6eeac7dad085ffcc829ccfd721f7b4ce39edcf7); ngspice ngspice-47 : Circuit level simulation program; klt klt 0.9.3.dev4260+gad8f069464a3
- **Execution** (per figure, each ONE `klt sim` corner-matrix request over the passive axes that figure responds to, DR-0009):
  - power: 45 cells; `klt sim` backend `aws-batch-fleet`, job id `klt-sim-c19ce71f496b`, Spot m6i.4xlarge, runner klt `0.9.0` vs client `0.9.3.dev4260+gad8f069464a3` (compatibility `match`)
  - slew: 405 cells; `klt sim` backend `aws-batch-fleet`, job id `klt-sim-4452f0da3090`, Spot c7i.4xlarge, runner klt `0.9.0` vs client `0.9.3.dev4260+gad8f069464a3` (compatibility `match`)
  - swing: 135 cells; `klt sim` backend `aws-batch-fleet`, job id `klt-sim-fb500080acb7`, Spot c7i.4xlarge, runner klt `0.9.0` vs client `0.9.3.dev4260+gad8f069464a3` (compatibility `match`)
- **DUT**: `design/netlist/opamp_two_stage.spice` (normalised sha256 `33b58dac62c747de979b33423bb54148cab3eb334fd9fab6051b7ed99226e41b`); snapshot `netlist-snapshots/20261123-213924-a25bbbc.spice`
- **Points**: typical / -40 C / 2.97 V; typical / -40 C / 3.30 V; typical / -40 C / 3.63 V; typical / 27 C / 2.97 V; typical / 27 C / 3.30 V; typical / 27 C / 3.63 V; typical / 125 C / 2.97 V; typical / 125 C / 3.30 V; typical / 125 C / 3.63 V; ff / -40 C / 2.97 V; ff / -40 C / 3.30 V; ff / -40 C / 3.63 V; ff / 27 C / 2.97 V; ff / 27 C / 3.30 V; ff / 27 C / 3.63 V; ff / 125 C / 2.97 V; ff / 125 C / 3.30 V; ff / 125 C / 3.63 V; ss / -40 C / 2.97 V; ss / -40 C / 3.30 V; ss / -40 C / 3.63 V; ss / 27 C / 2.97 V; ss / 27 C / 3.30 V; ss / 27 C / 3.63 V; ss / 125 C / 2.97 V; ss / 125 C / 3.30 V; ss / 125 C / 3.63 V; fs / -40 C / 2.97 V; fs / -40 C / 3.30 V; fs / -40 C / 3.63 V; fs / 27 C / 2.97 V; fs / 27 C / 3.30 V; fs / 27 C / 3.63 V; fs / 125 C / 2.97 V; fs / 125 C / 3.30 V; fs / 125 C / 3.63 V; sf / -40 C / 2.97 V; sf / -40 C / 3.30 V; sf / -40 C / 3.63 V; sf / 27 C / 2.97 V; sf / 27 C / 3.30 V; sf / 27 C / 3.63 V; sf / 125 C / 2.97 V; sf / 125 C / 3.30 V; sf / 125 C / 3.63 V, 45 points; ibias = 10 uA, same benches and extraction as the 45-point grid.
- **Passive axes per figure** (DR-0009, issue #322): power sweeps neither passive axis (1 combination(s) per point, 45 cells); slew sweeps RZ x CC (9 combination(s) per point, 405 cells); swing sweeps RZ (3 combination(s) per point, 135 cells).
- **Invariance asserted, not re-derived (power)**: RZ and CC are held at `typical` for power. Basis: the two full-scope 3x3 records `20261018-142357-91dab49` and `20261011-052206-c635487` (each 45 / 105 / 405 distinct power / swing / slew results over 405 cells) showed power invariant to both RZ and CC and swing invariant to CC; see `spec/decision-records/0009-per-figure-passive-scope.md`. The shared helpers keep the uniform 3x3 as their default (`pc.ALL_PASSIVE_AXES`), so a full audit needs no helper change.
- **Invariance asserted, not re-derived (swing)**: CC is held at `typical` for swing. Basis: the two full-scope 3x3 records `20261018-142357-91dab49` and `20261011-052206-c635487` (each 45 / 105 / 405 distinct power / swing / slew results over 405 cells) showed power invariant to both RZ and CC and swing invariant to CC; see `spec/decision-records/0009-per-figure-passive-scope.md`. The shared helpers keep the uniform 3x3 as their default (`pc.ALL_PASSIVE_AXES`), so a full audit needs no helper change.
- **Passive-section policy (swept)**: RZ (`ppolyf_u_1k`) uses `res_typical` / `res_ff` (best, 0.8x) / `res_ss` (worst, 1.2x); CC (`cap_mim_2f0_m4m5_noshield`) uses `mimcap_typical` / `mimcap_ff` (best, 0.9x) / `mimcap_ss` (worst, 1.1x). Every combination of the axes a figure sweeps runs independently at each point (a dropped axis stays `typical`). Each cell's generated deck was checked to load exactly the sections its name encodes. "best"/"worst" are the PDK ff/ss labels, not a claim about which is worse for a row; the tables decide.
- **Comparison to `20261018-142357-91dab49` (DR-0009 verification)**: 45/45 power `.dat` and 135/135 swing `.dat` files are byte-identical (exact same size and sha256; see "Stored evidence" below) and all printed power and swing values match identically. Across all 405 slew cells (toolchain moved ngspice-42 -> ngspice-47), 402/405 printed values match identically to 2 decimal places; 3/405 differ by 0.01 V/us (<= 0.066% relative): `ff / -40 C / 3.63 V` (RZ typical, CC typical: 16.69 -> 16.68 V/us), `ff / -40 C / 3.30 V` (RZ best, CC worst: 15.06 -> 15.05 V/us), and `ff / -40 C / 3.30 V` (RZ worst, CC best: 18.54 -> 18.53 V/us).

## Controls (single local units at the nominal point, typical passives)

| Control | Condition | Power (uW) | Slew (V/us) | Swing (Vpp) | note |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `nominal` | unmodified nominal point (reference for the controls) | 273.7 | 16.15 | 2.893 |  |
| `ibias-half` | ibias driven at 5 uA instead of 10 uA (all else nominal) | 137.1 | 8.09 | 2.997 |  |
| `ibias-zero` | ibias driven at 0 A (no bias current; all else nominal) | 0.0 | INVALID | INVALID | slew: output did not settle to the input at the pre-rise instant (vout 1.612 V vs vinp 1.150 V); swing: mid-range gain 0.933 is not -1 +- 0.05 (wrong polarity/feedback) |

Required behaviour (for the figures run): ibias-half lowers both power (< 0.75x) and slew (< 0.8x); ibias-zero never passes slew or swing and draws < 0.1x the nominal power.
